# Third-Party Notices

Depthcut is licensed under MIT OR Apache-2.0 (see `LICENSE-MIT` and `LICENSE-APACHE`).
It is built on the open-source software listed below. This file is generated by
`scripts/gen_third_party_notices.py` from the actual Windows build graph — do not edit it by hand.

## FFmpeg (not bundled)

Depthcut does **not** include FFmpeg. It runs `ffmpeg.exe` / `ffprobe.exe`
as separate programs. If you use *Download FFmpeg Automatically*, the editor
downloads the "release essentials" build published by gyan.dev
(<https://www.gyan.dev/ffmpeg/builds/>), verifies its SHA-256 and stores it in
`%LOCALAPPDATA%\Depthcut\bin`. That build is licensed under the
**GNU General Public License v3** (it includes GPL components such as x264 and
x265); its license text is saved beside it as `FFMPEG-LICENSE.txt`, and the
corresponding source code is available from <https://ffmpeg.org/download.html>
and the gyan.dev page above. FFmpeg is a trademark of Fabrice Bellard.

## Native libraries compiled in

- **whisper.cpp / ggml** — MIT — <https://github.com/ggml-org/whisper.cpp>. Speech recognition engine, compiled into the binary through whisper-rs-sys (its MIT text is listed under whisper-rs-sys below).

### Text 162 — used by: ring 0.17.14

```text
*ring* uses an "ISC" license, like BoringSSL used to use, for new code
files. See LICENSE-other-bits for the text of that license.

See LICENSE-BoringSSL for code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the
Apache 2.0 license. Some code that was sourced from BoringSSL under the ISC
license. In each case, the license info is at the top of the file.

See src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-APACHE and src/polyfill/once_cell/LICENSE-MIT
for the license to code that was sourced from the once_cell project.
```
